UEFA Country Coefficients 1955/1956
> 1957
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1955/1956, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1959/1960, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1962 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1962/1963
> 1964
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1962/1963, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 1963/1964
> 1965
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1963/1964, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1965 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1965/1966
> 1967
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1965/1966, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1967/1968, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. 1968 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1968/1969
> 1970
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1968/1969, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 1969/1970
> 1971
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1969/1970, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1971 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1971/1972
> 1973
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1971/1972, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1972 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1972/1973
> 1974
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1972/1973, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 1973/1974
> 1975
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1973/1974, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1975 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1975/1976
> 1977
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1975/1976, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1976 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1976/1977
> 1978
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1976/1977, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 1977/1978
> 1979
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1977/1978, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1979 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1979/1980
> 1981
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1979/1980, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1980 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1980/1981
> 1982
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1980/1981, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 1981/1982
> 1983
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1981/1982, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1983 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1983/1984
> 1985
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1983/1984, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1984 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1984/1985
> 1986
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1984/1985, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1985 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1985/1986
> 1987
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1985/1986, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1987 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1987/1988
> 1989
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1987/1988, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1988 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1988/1989
> 1990
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1988/1989, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 1989/1990
> 1991
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1989/1990, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1991 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1991/1992
> 1993
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1991/1992, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1992 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1992/1993
> 1994
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1992/1993, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1993 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1993/1994
> 1995
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1993/1994, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1994 <
UEFA Country Coefficients (method=1) 1994/1995
> 1996
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1994/1995, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1995 <
UEFA Country Coefficients (method=1) 1995/1996
> 1997
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1995/1996, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1996 <
UEFA Country Coefficients (method=1) 1996/1997
> 1998
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1996/1997, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1997 <
UEFA Country Coefficients (method=1) 1997/1998
> 1999
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1997/1998, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1998 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1998/1999
> 2000
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1998/1999, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

1999 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 1999/2000
> 2001
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 1999/2000, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2000 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2000/2001
> 2002
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2000/2001, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2001 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2001/2002
> 2003
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2001/2002, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2002 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2002/2003
> 2004
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2002/2003, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2003 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2003/2004
> 2005
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2003/2004, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2004 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2004/2005
> 2006
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2004/2005, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2005 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2005/2006
> 2007
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2005/2006, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2006 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2006/2007
> 2008
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2006/2007, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2007 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2007/2008
> 2009
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2007/2008, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2008 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2008/2009
> 2010
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2008/2009, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2009/2010
> 2011
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2009/2010, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2010 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2010/2011
> 2012
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2010/2011, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2011 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2011/2012
> 2013
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2011/2012, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2012 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2012/2013
> 2014
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2012/2013, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.



Cup	qW	qD	qL	#W	#D	#L	Bonus	Points	Average
1	
Germany
7 teams
6	1	1	33	20	15	33	125.5	17.928
1	Bayern München	CL	0	0	0	10	1	2	12	33.0

2013 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2013/2014
> 2015
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2013/2014, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2014 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2014/2015
> 2016
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2014/2015, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2015/2016
> 2017
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2015/2016, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2016/2017
> 2018
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2016/2017, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2017/2018
> 2019
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2017/2018, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

2018 <
UEFA Country Coefficients 2018/2019
> 2020
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2018/2019, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.



Cup	qW	qD	qL	#W	#D	#L	Bonus	Points	Average
1	
England

UEFA Country Coefficients 2019/2020
> 2021
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2019/2020, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2020/2021
> 2022
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2020/2021, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2021/2022
> 2023
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2021/2022, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2022/2023
> 2024
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2022/2023, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2023/2024
> 2025
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2023/2024, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2024/2025
> 2026
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2024/2025, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.

UEFA Country Coefficients 2025/2026
> 2027
This page shows the calculation of the UEFA country coefficients in the season 2025/2026, which are determined by the number of points divided by the number of participating clubs. The coefficients of 5 successive years are used for the UEFA country ranking. The columns qW, qD, and qL show the number of wins, draws, and losses in the qualifying rounds. The columns #W, #D, and #L show the number of wins, draws, and losses excluding the qualifying rounds. The sequence of the individual clubs is based on the number of points, and secondly on the number of matches. For all details see Calculation of coefficients.
